4552                    let mut resources = Resources::new(dettid);
4553                    resources.insert(ResourceID::WaitidSignals(signals), Permission::W);
4554                    self.force_unblock_thread(dettid, resources);
4555                }
4556                Some(WaitidSignalRequest::Pending(existing)) => {
4557                    signals.extend(existing);
4558                    signals.sort_by_key(SigWrapper::raw);
4559                    signals.dedup();
4560                    // One resource, always. `step4_resource_block` and
4561                    // `blocking_request_is_ready` both assert a request carries
4562                    // exactly one, so this must replace the request rather than
4563                    // add to it. Only a request whose sole resource is already
4564                    // `WaitidSignals` reaches here, so nothing is discarded.
4565                    let mut resources = Resources::new(dettid);
4566                    resources.insert(ResourceID::WaitidSignals(signals), Permission::W);
4567                    let Some(next_turn) = self.next_turns.get_mut(&dettid) else {
4568                        continue;
4569                    };
4570                    next_turn.req = Ivar::full(Ok(resources));
4571                }
4572                None => {
4573                    let Some(existing) = self.restartable_internal_io_signals(dettid) else {
4574                        continue;
4575                    };
4576                    // ERESTARTSYS is the existing contract by which the kernel
4577                    // decides whether this physically pending signal is
4578                    // blocked, ignored, restarts the syscall, or interrupts it
4579                    // for a handler. Replace the polling request rather than
4580                    // adding a second resource; step4 accepts exactly one.
4581                    if self.run_queue.contains_tid(dettid) {
4582                        let removed = self.run_queue.remove_tid(dettid);
4583                        debug_assert!(
4584                            removed,
4585                            "run_queue.contains_tid disagreed with remove_tid for {dettid}"
4586                        );
4587                    }
4588                    signals.extend(existing);
4589                    signals.sort_by_key(SigWrapper::raw);
4590                    signals.dedup();
4591                    if signals.is_empty() {
4592                        continue;
4593                    }
4594                    let mut resources = Resources::new(dettid);
4595                    // `WaitidSignals` is already the scheduler's one-resource
4596                    // representation for a complete signal identity set. The
4597                    // ERESTARTSYS marker distinguishes this write wakeup from a
4598                    // waitid request and survives later notification batches.
4599                    resources.insert(ResourceID::WaitidSignals(signals), Permission::W);
4600                    resources.set_signal_interrupt_errno(Errno::ERESTARTSYS);
4601                    self.force_unblock_thread(dettid, resources);
4602                }
4603            }
4604        }
4605    }
4606
4607    /// Drain removals deferred by [`Scheduler::deschedule_or_defer`] at the same
4608    /// deterministic `step2` point as admissions, and *before* them, so a thread
4609    /// killed while an admission was still buffered is not re-enqueued. The
4610    /// window is closed here (`tentative_selection` is `None`), so
4611    /// `remove_tid`'s guard holds. The `BTreeMap` makes removal order canonical;
4612    /// each disposition determines whether a same-raw-TID admission is stale or
4613    /// is the explicitly paired exec replacement.
4614    fn drain_pending_run_queue_removals(&mut self) {
4615        if self.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty() {
4616            return;
4617        }
4618        let pending = std::mem::take(&mut self.pending_run_queue_removals);
4619        for (dtid, disposition) in pending {
4620            match disposition {
4621                RemovalDisposition::Retire => {
4622                    // Ordinary logical death cancels a buffered admission for
4623                    // the same thread incarnation.
4624                    self.pending_run_queue_admissions.remove(&dtid);
4625                }
4626                RemovalDisposition::ReplaceThenAdmit => {
4627                    assert!(
4628                        self.next_turns.contains_key(&dtid),
4629                        "exec replacement {:?} lost its scheduler registration",
4630                        dtid
4631                    );
4632                    assert!(
4633                        self.pending_run_queue_admissions.contains_key(&dtid),
4634                        "exec replacement {:?} lost its paired admission",
4635                        dtid
4636                    );
4637                    assert!(
4638                        !self.thread_is_logically_killed(dtid),
4639                        "logically dead exec replacement {:?} reached the drain",
4640                        dtid
4641                    );
4642                }
4643            }
4644            // Always remove the old physical queue slot before a replacement
4645            // admission is applied. This prevents the new image from inheriting
4646            // the destroyed leader's round-robin position.
4647            let _ = self.run_queue.remove_tid(dtid);
4648        }
4649    }
4650
4651    /// Push `dtid` onto the run queue immediately, idempotently: a thread
4652    /// already queued is left in place rather than enqueued twice.
4653    fn admit_now(&mut self, dtid: DetTid, side: AdmitSide) {
4654        if self.run_queue.contains_tid(dtid) {
4655            return;
4656        }
4657        match side {
4658            AdmitSide::Front => {
4659                let _ = self.runqueue_push_front(dtid);
4660            }
4661            AdmitSide::Back => {
4662                let _ = self.runqueue_push_back(dtid);
4663            }
4664        }
4665    }
4666
4667    /// Drain admissions deferred by [`Scheduler::admit_to_run_queue`] into the
4668    /// run queue at a single deterministic point: the very start of `step2`,
4669    /// before `step3_peek` opens a tentative window (so `tentative_selection` is
4670    /// guaranteed `None` here). Draining a `BTreeMap` visits `DetTid`s in sorted
4671    /// order, so the resulting run-queue state is a pure function of the
4672    /// deterministic schedule rather than of RPC/lock-acquisition timing.
4673    fn drain_pending_run_queue_admissions(&mut self) {
4674        if self.pending_run_queue_admissions.is_empty() {
4675            return;
4676        }
4677        let pending = std::mem::take(&mut self.pending_run_queue_admissions);
4678        // `pending` is a `BTreeMap`, so iteration visits `DetTid`s in sorted
4679        // order. Resolving each intent here (rather than at the racing handler)
4680        // means any `RunsPostFork::Random` PRNG draw is consumed in this
4681        // canonical order, so both the admission order *and* the chosen side are
4682        // pure functions of deterministic state.
4683        for (dtid, intent) in pending {
4684            // A thread retired (exec/kill) between record and drain is skipped.
4685            // `remove_blocking_entries` also clears the buffer on teardown, so
4686            // this guard is defensive against any teardown path that does not.
4687            if !self.next_turns.contains_key(&dtid) {
4688                trace!(
4689                    "[step2] skipping deferred admission of retired thread {:?}",
4690                    dtid
4691                );
4692                continue;
4693            }
4694            let side = self.resolve_admit_intent(intent);
4695            self.admit_now(dtid, side);
4696        }
4697    }
4698
4699    /// Decide which side gets the first post-fork turn for an ordinary clone.
4700    pub(crate) fn child_runs_first_post_fork(&mut self, mode: RunsPostFork) -> bool {
4701        match mode {
4702            RunsPostFork::Child => true,
4703            RunsPostFork::Parent => false,
4704            RunsPostFork::Random => self.post_fork_prng.random(),
4705        }
4706    }
4707
4708    /// Check if a thread is alive, but removed from run queue.
4709    fn thread_status(&self, dtid: DetTid) -> ThreadStatus {
4710        if self.run_queue.contains_tid(dtid) {
4711            ThreadStatus::Running
4712        } else {
4713            // Check all the places a blocked thread could be hiding.
4714            // TODO: this O(N) search could be made more efficient with more indexing structures.
4715            for v in self.blocked.futex_waiters.values() {
4716                for waiter in v {
4717                    if waiter.dettid == dtid {
4718                        return ThreadStatus::NotRunning;
4719                    }
4720                }
4721            }
4722            for (_, evt) in self.blocked.timed_waiters.iter() {
4723                match evt {
4724                    TimedEvent::ThreadEvt(dt) => {
4725                        if dt == dtid {
4726                            return ThreadStatus::NotRunning;
4727                        }
4728                    }
4729                    TimedEvent::SignalEvt(_, _, _) => {}
4730                }
4731            }
4732            if self.blocked.external_io_blockers.contains_key(&dtid) {
4733                return ThreadStatus::NotRunning;
4734            }
4735            if self.blocked.rt_sigsuspend_blockers.contains_key(&dtid)
4736                || self.blocked.child_waiters.contains_key(&dtid)
4737                || self
4738                    .blocked
4739                    .physical_child_waiters
4740                    .values()
4741                    .any(|waiters| waiters.contains(&dtid))
4742            {
4743                return ThreadStatus::NotRunning;
4744            }
4745            if self.pending_run_queue_admissions.contains_key(&dtid) {
4746                return ThreadStatus::Running;
4747            }
4748            ThreadStatus::Gone
4749        }
4750    }
4751
4752    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
4753    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(#252): Confirm completed and final partial slices belong in one distribution.
4754    /// Fold an exiting thread's completed-timeslice distribution into the
4755    /// scheduler's per-thread record, to be reported in the final run summary.
4756    pub fn record_timeslice_stats(&mut self, dettid: DetTid, stats: TimesliceStats) {
4757        if stats.is_empty() {
4758            return;
4759        }
4760        self.per_thread_timeslice
4761            .entry(dettid)
4762            .or_default()
4763            .merge(&stats);
4764    }
4765
4766    /// Record an exiting thread's final completed-syscall count.
4767    pub fn record_syscall_count(&mut self, dettid: DetTid, count: u64) {
4768        self.per_thread_syscalls.insert(dettid, count);
4769    }
4770
4771    /// Summarize the run after completion, as a RunSummary. This is partial because the Scheduler
4772    /// does not have all the necessary information.
4773    ///
4774    /// Side Effects: This also flushes the in-memory PreemptionWriter to disk.
4775    pub fn generate_partial_run_summary(
4776        &mut self,
4777        preemptions_to: Option<&PathBuf>,
4778    ) -> anyhow::Result<RunSummary> {
4779        let schedevent_replayed = self
4780            .replayer
4781            .as_ref()
4782            .map(|r| r.events_popped)
4783            .unwrap_or_default();
4784        let total_desync_stats = self
4785            .replayer
4786            .as_ref()
4787            .map(|r| {
4788                r.desync_counts
4789                    .values()
4790                    .fold(Default::default(), |x: DesyncStats, y: &DesyncStats| x + *y)
4791            })
4792            .unwrap_or_default();
4793
4794        let total_desyncs = total_desync_stats.soft + total_desync_stats.hard;
4795        let desync_descrip = if total_desyncs > 0 {
4796            let mut buf = String::new();
4797            write!(
4798                buf,
4799                "  Encountered {} soft desyncs, {} hard (with {} at context switch points), {} resyncs ({}/{} insertion/deletion).\n  Per thread (soft,hard,@switch,resync): ",
4800                total_desync_stats.soft,
4801                total_desync_stats.hard,
4802                total_desync_stats.at_context_switch,
4803                total_desync_stats.resync_insertions + total_desync_stats.resync_deletions,
4804                total_desync_stats.resync_insertions,
4805                total_desync_stats.resync_deletions,
4806            )?;
4807            if let Some(ref replayer) = self.replayer {
4808                for (tid, desync_stats) in replayer.desync_counts.iter() {
4809                    write!(
4810                        buf,
4811                        "{}=>({},{},{},{}) ",
4812                        tid,
4813                        desync_stats.soft,
4814                        desync_stats.hard,
4815                        desync_stats.at_context_switch,
4816                        desync_stats.resync_insertions + desync_stats.resync_deletions,
4817                    )?;
4818                }
4819            }
4820            writeln!(buf)?;
4821            Some(buf)
4822        } else {
4823            None
4824        };
4825
4826        let reprio_descrip = if let Some(pw) = self.preemption_writer.take() {
4827            let mut buf = String::new();
4828            writeln!(
4829                buf,
4830                "Record of {} preemption and reprioritization events:",
4831                pw.len()
4832            )?;
4833            if let Some(path) = preemptions_to {
4834                writeln!(buf, "  (Writing to file {:?})", path)?;
4835                if let Err(str) = pw.flush() {
4836                    tracing::warn!("{}", str);
4837                }
4838            } else {
4839                // Recording, but not outputting to file, so this is the only (partial) record of it:
4840                writeln!(buf, "{}", truncated(200, pw.into_string()))?;
4841            }
4842            Some(buf)
4843        } else {
4844            None
4845        };
4846
4847        let num_processes = self.thread_tree.thread_group_leaders.len() as u64;
4848        let num_threads = self.thread_tree.size() as u64;
4849        let threads_descrip = format!("{}", self.thread_tree);
4850
4851        // Aggregate the per-thread timeslice distributions collected at thread
4852        // exit. BTreeMap gives a deterministic (dettid-sorted) ordering.
4853        let per_thread_timeslice: Vec<(DetTid, TimesliceStats)> = self
4854            .per_thread_timeslice
4855            .iter()
4856            .map(|(k, v)| (*k, *v))
4857            .collect();
4858        let mut timeslice_stats = TimesliceStats::default();
4859        for (_, st) in &per_thread_timeslice {
4860            timeslice_stats.merge(st);
4861        }
4862        let syscalls = self.per_thread_syscalls.values().copied().sum();
4863
4864        Ok(RunSummary {
4865            sched_turns: self.turn,
4866            schedevent_replayed,
4867            schedevent_recorded: self.recorded_event_count,
4868            schedevent_desynced: total_desyncs,
4869            // schedevent_desynced_at_context_switch: total_desyncs.at_context_switch,
4870            desync_descrip,
4871            reprio_descrip,
4872            threads_descrip,
4873            num_processes,
4874            num_threads,
4875            syscalls: Some(syscalls),
4876            virttime_elapsed: 0, // Cannot fill.
4877            virttime_final: 0,   // Cannot fill.
4878            realtime_elapsed: None,
4879            timeslice_stats,
4880            per_thread_timeslice,
4881        })
4882    }
4883
4884    /// Summarize the state of the scheduler while executing (verbose).
4885    pub fn full_summary(&self) -> String {
4886        let mut buf = String::new();
4887        write!(&mut buf, "  {}", self.run_queue).unwrap();
4888
4889        let total_futex_blocked: usize = self.blocked.futex_waiters.iter().map(|v| v.1.len()).sum();
4890        writeln!(
4891            &mut buf,
4892            "\n  Futex-waiters, {} blocked on {} futexes:",
4893            total_futex_blocked,
4894            self.blocked.futex_waiters.len()
4895        )
4896        .unwrap();
4897        // Sorted, and no `Ivar` Debug. `format_terminal_deadlock` above already
4898        // documents the two sources this avoids, and this function had neither
4899        // guard: `futex_waiters` is a `HashMap`, so it iterates in a randomized
4900        // order once it holds more than one entry, and `FutexWaiter`'s derived
4901        // `Debug` prints its `Ivar`'s parked waker as raw host pointers.
4902        //
4903        // Measured before this change, three runs of
4904        // `--stop-after-turn=15 -- rustbin_futex_and_print`, which reaches this
4905        // function through the `--stop-after-turn` warning:
4906        //   Waker { data: 0x564ea2a82c80
4907        //   Waker { data: 0x55f2120b9c80
4908        //   Waker { data: 0x56163deeec80
4909        // Three runs, three host addresses, in a WARN record that
4910        // `--verify-strict` compares.
4911        //
4912        // Only guest-level identities are printed here: the futex key, the
4913        // waiting dettid and the bitset are all values Detcore already
4914        // determinizes.
4915        let mut futex_rows: Vec<String> = self
4916            .blocked
4917            .futex_waiters
4918            .iter()
4919            .map(|(futex, waiters)| {
4920                let mut dettids: Vec<String> =
4921                    waiters.iter().map(|w| w.dettid.to_string()).collect();
4922                dettids.sort();
4923                let mut bitsets: Vec<u32> = waiters.iter().map(|w| w.bitset).collect();
4924                bitsets.sort_unstable();
4925                format!(
4926                    "    {:?} => {} waiter(s), dettids [{}], bitsets {:?}",
4927                    futex,
4928                    waiters.len(),
4929                    dettids.join(", "),
4930                    bitsets
4931                )
4932            })
4933            .collect();
4934        futex_rows.sort();
4935        for row in futex_rows {
4936            writeln!(&mut buf, "{}", row).unwrap();
4937        }
4938
4939        writeln!(
4940            &mut buf,
4941            "\n  Timed-waiters, {}:",
4942            self.blocked.timed_waiters.len()
4943        )
4944        .unwrap();
4945        for (time, dtid) in self.blocked.timed_waiters.iter() {
4946            writeln!(&mut buf, "    {} => {}", time, dtid).unwrap();
4947        }
4948
4949        writeln!(
4950            &mut buf,
4951            "\n  External-IO-blocked, {}:",
4952            self.blocked.external_io_blockers.len(),
4953        )
4954        .unwrap();
4955        for x in &self.blocked.external_io_blockers {
4956            writeln!(&mut buf, "    {:?}", x).unwrap();
4957        }
4958
4959        writeln!(
4960            &mut buf,
4961            "\n  Rt-sigsuspend-blocked, {}:",
4962            self.blocked.rt_sigsuspend_blockers.len(),
4963        )
4964        .unwrap();
4965        for x in &self.blocked.rt_sigsuspend_blockers {
4966            writeln!(&mut buf, "    {:?}", x).unwrap();
4967        }
4968
4969        writeln!(&mut buf, "\n  Next_turns: ").unwrap();
4970        for (dtid, nxt) in self.next_turns.iter() {
4971            writeln!(
4972                &mut buf,
4973                " ==> dtid {}, req {}, resp {}",
4974                dtid, nxt.req, nxt.resp
4975            )
4976            .unwrap();
4977        }
4978        buf
4979    }
4980
4981    // Return whether we should print the stacktrace after recording this event.
4982    // This is redundant with the consume_schedevent logic but allows us to print on either
4983    // recording or replay.
4984    pub fn record_event(&mut self, ev: &SchedEvent) -> MaybePrintStack {
4985        debug!(
4986            "[dtid {}] Record scheduled event #{}: {:?}",
4987            &ev.dettid, self.recorded_event_count, ev
4988        );
4989        let pw = self
4990            .preemption_writer
4991            .as_mut()
4992            .expect("trace_schedevent should be called only when preemption_writer is set");
4993        pw.insert_schedevent(ev.clone());
4994
4995        let print_stack = self.try_pop_stacktrace_event(self.recorded_event_count, ev);
4996        self.recorded_event_count += 1;
4997        print_stack
4998    }

5000    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
5001    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(#663)
5002    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(#869)
5003    // Returns the logical duration until any previously scheduled alarm, if any (zero otherwise).
5004    pub fn register_alarm(
5005        &mut self,
5006        detpid: DetPid,
5007        dettid: DetTid,
5008        now: LogicalTime,
5009        duration: LogicalTime,
5010        interval: LogicalTime,
5011        sig: Signal,
5012    ) -> (LogicalTime, LogicalTime) {
5013        let old = if duration == LogicalTime::ZERO {
5014            // Alarm of 0 cancels any pending signal.
5015            self.blocked.timed_waiters.remove_alarm(detpid)
5016        } else {
5017            let target_time = now + duration;
5018            self.blocked
5019                .timed_waiters
5020                .insert_alarm(target_time, detpid, dettid, sig, interval)
5021        };
5022        if let Some((old_target_time, old_interval)) = old {
5023            let remain_ns: u64 = old_target_time.as_nanos().saturating_sub(now.as_nanos());
5024            (LogicalTime::from_nanos(remain_ns), old_interval)
5025        } else {
5026            // Return 0 if no previous alarm, as per https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man2/alarm.2.html
5027            (LogicalTime::ZERO, LogicalTime::ZERO)
5028        }
5029    }
5030
5031    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
5032    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(#869)
5033    pub fn register_posix_timer(
5034        &mut self,
5035        detpid: DetPid,
5036        dettid: DetTid,
5037        timer_id: i32,
5038        deadline: Option<LogicalTime>,
5039        interval: LogicalTime,
5040        sig: Signal,
5041    ) {
5042        if let Some(deadline) = deadline {
5043            self.blocked
5044                .timed_waiters
5045                .insert_posix_timer(deadline, detpid, dettid, timer_id, sig, interval);
5046        } else {
5047            self.blocked
5048                .timed_waiters
5049                .remove_posix_timer(detpid, timer_id);
5050        }
5051    }
5052
5053    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
5054    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-841): Review logical ITIMER_REAL state queries.
5055    pub fn alarm_remaining(&self, detpid: DetPid, now: LogicalTime) -> LogicalTime {
5056        self.blocked
5057            .timed_waiters
5058            .alarm_time(detpid)
5059            .map(|deadline| {
5060                LogicalTime::from_nanos(deadline.as_nanos().saturating_sub(now.as_nanos()))
5061            })
5062            .unwrap_or(LogicalTime::ZERO)
5063    }
5064}

5106    #[test]
5107    fn chaos_pick_is_seed_deterministic_and_covers_all_choices() {
5108        let choices = [10, 20, 30, 40];
5109
5110        // Same seed => identical sequence of picks (the reproducibility that
5111        // targeted chaos relies on).
5112        let mut a = Pcg64Mcg::seed_from_u64(1234);
5113        let mut b = Pcg64Mcg::seed_from_u64(1234);
5114        for _ in 0..64 {
5115            assert_eq!(chaos_pick(&mut a, &choices), chaos_pick(&mut b, &choices));
5116        }
5117
5118        // Over enough draws every choice is reachable (the bias actually explores
5119        // the space rather than pinning one option).
5120        let mut prng = Pcg64Mcg::seed_from_u64(9);
5121        let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
5122        for _ in 0..256 {
5123            seen.insert(chaos_pick(&mut prng, &choices).unwrap());
5124        }
5125        assert_eq!(
5126            seen,
5127            choices.iter().copied().collect(),
5128            "chaos_pick should be able to return every choice"
5129        );
5130
5131        // An empty slice yields None rather than panicking.
5132        assert_eq!(chaos_pick(&mut prng, &[] as &[i32]), None);
5133
5134        // The front/back coin flip used by force_unblock_thread reaches both.
5135        let mut prng = Pcg64Mcg::seed_from_u64(7);
5136        let mut both = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
5137        for _ in 0..64 {
5138            both.insert(chaos_pick(&mut prng, &[true, false]).unwrap());
5139        }
5140        assert_eq!(both, [false, true].into_iter().collect());
5141    }

5286    fn install_runnable_exec_group(
5287        sched: &mut Scheduler,
5288        leader: DetTid,
5289        caller: DetTid,
5290    ) -> (DetPid, MmId, Ivar<SchedRequest>) {
5291        let detpid = DetPid::from_raw(leader.as_raw());
5292        let pre_exec_mm = MmId::initial(detpid);
5293        sched.thread_tree.add_child(leader, leader, true);
5294        sched.thread_tree.add_child(leader, caller, false);
5295        register_known_thread(sched, leader);
5296        register_known_thread(sched, caller);
5297        sched.runqueue_push_back(leader);
5298        sched.runqueue_push_back(caller);
5299        let old_leader_request = sched.next_turns.get(&leader).unwrap().req.clone();
5300        (detpid, pre_exec_mm, old_leader_request)
5301    }

5321    /// F1/F2: an admission deferred while a tentative_pop window is live must
5322    /// resolve its side -- including the `RunsPostFork::Random` PRNG draw -- at
5323    /// the `DetTid`-ordered drain, so the drained run queue is a pure function of
5324    /// deterministic state, independent of the order in which racing handlers
5325    /// buffered the admissions.
5326    #[test]
5327    fn deferred_admission_side_is_arrival_order_independent() {
5328        let config = Config {
5329            sched_seed: Some(0xABCD),
5330            runs_post_fork: RunsPostFork::Random,
5331            ..Default::default()
5332        };
5333        let lower = DetTid::from_raw(21);
5334        let higher = DetTid::from_raw(23);
5335
5336        // Buffer the two children in `order`, optionally while a tentative
5337        // window is live, then drain. Return the drained relative order of the
5338        // two children (the anchor is filtered out because it is consumed by the
5339        // window in the `open_window` case but not otherwise -- what must be
5340        // deterministic is the children's order and chosen sides).
5341        let drained_order = |order: [DetTid; 2], open_window: bool| -> Vec<DetTid> {
5342            let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&config);
5343            let anchor = DetTid::from_raw(5);
5344            register_known_thread(&mut sched, anchor);
5345            sched.runqueue_push_back(anchor);
5346            register_known_thread(&mut sched, lower);
5347            register_known_thread(&mut sched, higher);
5348
5349            if open_window {
5350                assert_eq!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_next(), Some(anchor));
5351                assert!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress());
5352            }
5353            for tid in order {
5354                sched.admit_to_run_queue(tid, AdmitIntent::PostFork(RunsPostFork::Random));
5355            }
5356            // Admission ALWAYS defers -- window or not -- so nothing is pushed or
5357            // resolved (no side chosen, no PRNG drawn) until the drain.
5358            assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.contains_key(&lower));
5359            assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.contains_key(&higher));
5360            assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(lower));
5361            assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(higher));
5362
5363            if open_window {
5364                let _ = sched.run_queue.commit_tentative_pop();
5365            }
5366            sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions();
5367            sched
5368                .run_queue
5369                .tids()
5370                .copied()
5371                .filter(|t| *t == lower || *t == higher)
5372                .collect()
5373        };
5374
5375        // The drained order/side is independent of BOTH host-timing inputs the
5376        // old immediate path was sensitive to: the arrival (buffering) order of
5377        // the racing handlers, and whether a tentative window happened to be
5378        // live when each handler ran.
5379        let baseline = drained_order([lower, higher], true);
5380        assert_eq!(
5381            baseline,
5382            drained_order([higher, lower], true),
5383            "arrival order"
5384        );
5385        assert_eq!(
5386            baseline,
5387            drained_order([lower, higher], false),
5388            "window state"
5389        );
5390        assert_eq!(baseline, drained_order([higher, lower], false), "both");
5391        assert!(baseline.contains(&lower) && baseline.contains(&higher));
5392    }
5393
5394    #[test]
5395    fn robust_list_wake_batches_are_arrival_order_independent() {
5396        let lower = DetTid::from_raw(21);
5397        let higher = DetTid::from_raw(23);
5398        let first_owner = DetTid::from_raw(31);
5399        let second_owner = DetTid::from_raw(33);
5400        let mm = MmId::initial(DetTid::from_raw(5));
5401        let lower_futex = FutexID::private(mm, 0x404100);
5402        let higher_futex = FutexID::private(mm, 0x404200);
5403
5404        let deliver = |reverse: bool| {
5405            let mut first = ThreadState::<()>::new(first_owner, &Config::default(), ());
5406            let mut second = first.clone();
5407            second.dettid = second_owner;
5408            first.record_robust_list_head(Some(0x404100));
5409            second.record_robust_list_head(Some(0x404200));
5410            first.stage_robust_list_wakes(
5411                RobustListExit::ExitGroup,
5412                vec![
5413                    (
5414                        second_owner,
5415                        vec![RobustListWake {
5416                            futex: higher_futex,
5417                        }],
5418                    ),
5419                    (first_owner, vec![RobustListWake { futex: lower_futex }]),
5420                ],
5421            );
5422            let first_time = DetTime::default();
5423            let mut second_time = first_time.clone();
5424            second_time.add_syscall();
5425            let ready = if reverse {
5426                assert_eq!(
5427                    second.take_robust_list_wakes_after_exit(None, second_time.clone()),
5428                    None,
5429                    "the first physical exit must not send a partial request"
5430                );
5431                first
5432                    .take_robust_list_wakes_after_exit(None, first_time)
5433                    .expect("the final physical exit must release the group")
5434                    .1
5435            } else {
5436                assert_eq!(
5437                    first.take_robust_list_wakes_after_exit(None, first_time),
5438                    None,
5439                    "the first physical exit must not send a partial request"
5440                );
5441                second
5442                    .take_robust_list_wakes_after_exit(None, second_time)
5443                    .expect("the final physical exit must release the group")
5444                    .1
5445            };
5446            let request: Vec<_> = ready
5447                .into_iter()
5448                .map(|(owner, wake)| (owner, wake.futex))
5449                .collect();
5450            assert_eq!(
5451                request,
5452                vec![(first_owner, lower_futex), (second_owner, higher_futex)],
5453                "one owner/futex-sorted request must carry the complete group"
5454            );
5455
5456            let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
5457            let anchor = DetTid::from_raw(5);
5458            register_known_thread(&mut sched, anchor);
5459            sched.runqueue_push_back(anchor);
5460            register_known_thread(&mut sched, lower);
5461            register_known_thread(&mut sched, higher);
5462            sched.sleep_futex_waiter(&lower, lower_futex, None, u32::MAX);
5463            sched.sleep_futex_waiter(&higher, higher_futex, None, u32::MAX);
5464
5465            assert_eq!(sched.wake_futex_waiters_after_exit(&request), vec![1, 1]);
5466
5467            let pending: Vec<_> = sched
5468                .pending_run_queue_admissions
5469                .iter()
5470                .map(|(tid, intent)| (*tid, *intent))
5471                .collect();
5472            assert_eq!(
5473                pending,
5474                vec![
5475                    (lower, AdmitIntent::Fixed(AdmitSide::Back)),
5476                    (higher, AdmitIntent::Fixed(AdmitSide::Back)),
5477                ]
5478            );
5479            assert_eq!(
5480                sched.run_queue.tids().copied().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
5481                vec![anchor],
5482                "physical-exit wakes must wait for the deterministic drain"
5483            );
5484
5485            sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions();
5486            let drained = sched.run_queue.tids().copied().collect::<Vec<_>>();
5487            assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.is_empty());
5488            (pending, drained)
5489        };
5490
5491        let forward = deliver(false);
5492        let reverse = deliver(true);
5493        assert_eq!(forward, reverse);
5494        assert_eq!(forward.1, vec![DetTid::from_raw(5), lower, higher]);
5495    }
5496
5497    /// F3: a run-queue removal requested while a tentative_pop window is live
5498    /// (an asynchronous exec reconnect racing the daemon) must be deferred, not
5499    /// applied through `remove_tid`'s `tentative_selection.is_none()` guard, and
5500    /// a pending-removal thread whose `next_turns` entry is already gone must not
5501    /// crash `are_all_quiesced`. The removal lands at the next drain.
5502    /// ITEM 1 REGRESSION: a NON-LEADER thread is live, even though it is not a
5503    /// kill target.
5504    ///
5505    /// `process_signal_targets` models `kill(2)`, whose first act is to refuse
5506    /// anything that is not a thread-group leader. `sched_setattr(2)` and its
5507    /// relatives resolve through `find_task_by_vpid`, which finds any live
5508    /// task. Answering the second question with the first resolver reports
5509    /// ESRCH for a thread that is plainly running, so the two must stay
5510    /// distinguishable -- this test fails the moment `thread_is_live` is
5511    /// reduced to the kill resolver.
5512    #[test]
5513    fn a_non_leader_thread_is_live_without_being_a_kill_target() {
5514        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
5515        let leader = DetTid::from_raw(11);
5516        let member = DetTid::from_raw(12);
5517        let stranger = DetTid::from_raw(13);
5518        sched.thread_tree.add_child(leader, leader, true);
5519        sched.thread_tree.add_child(leader, member, false);
5520        register_known_thread(&mut sched, leader);
5521        register_known_thread(&mut sched, member);
5522
5523        assert!(sched.thread_is_live(leader), "the leader is obviously live");
5524        assert!(
5525            sched.thread_is_live(member),
5526            "a non-leader thread of a live group is live; sched_setattr must reach it"
5527        );
5528        assert!(
5529            !sched.thread_is_live(stranger),
5530            "a tid that was never registered is not live"
5531        );
5532
5533        // The distinction being preserved: the kill resolver still declines the
5534        // non-leader, which is correct for kill(2) and wrong for sched_setattr.
5535        assert!(
5536            sched.process_signal_targets(member).is_empty(),
5537            "kill(2) semantics: a non-leader is not a signal target, which is \
5538             exactly why it is the wrong resolver for a tid lookup"
5539        );
5540        assert!(
5541            !sched.process_signal_targets(leader).is_empty(),
5542            "the leader is a signal target, so the fixture is wired correctly"
5543        );
5544    }
5545
5546    #[test]
5547    fn deferred_removal_survives_tentative_window_and_drains() {
5548        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
5549        let anchor = DetTid::from_raw(5);
5550        let victim = DetTid::from_raw(9);
5551        register_known_thread(&mut sched, anchor);
5552        register_known_thread(&mut sched, victim);
5553        sched.runqueue_push_back(anchor);
5554        sched.runqueue_push_back(victim);
5555
5556        // Daemon peeks the anchor and releases the lock (window open).
5557        assert_eq!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_next(), Some(anchor));
5558        assert!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress());
5559
5560        // A racing handler descheduling the victim must buffer, not panic.
5561        sched.deschedule_or_defer(victim);
5562        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.contains_key(&victim));
5563        assert!(
5564            sched.run_queue.contains_tid(victim),
5565            "removal is deferred, so the stale entry lingers until the drain"
5566        );
5567
5568        // The handler has also made the victim logically dead: its next_turns
5569        // entry is gone. Iterating quiescence must SKIP the victim (filtered by
5570        // pending_run_queue_removals) rather than panic in check_request on the
5571        // missing next_turns entry -- without the filter this call panics.
5572        sched.next_turns.remove(&victim);
5573        let _ = sched.are_all_quiesced();
5574
5575        // Close the window and drain: the victim is gone, the anchor remains.
5576        let _ = sched.run_queue.commit_tentative_pop();
5577        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_removals();
5578        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty());
5579        assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(victim));
5580    }
5581
5582    /// Always-defer invariant (codex finding 1/2): even with NO tentative window
5583    /// live, a global-request handler NEVER pushes or pops the run queue
5584    /// directly -- both admission and removal are buffered and take effect only
5585    /// at the deterministic step2 drain. This removes the host-timing-dependent
5586    /// immediate path: whichever daemon phase a handler happened to race, it only
5587    /// records intent, so the run-queue mutation is applied at one fixed point in
5588    /// DetTid order regardless of host arrival timing.
5589    #[test]
5590    fn run_queue_mutations_always_defer_to_the_drain() {
5591        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
5592        let keep = DetTid::from_raw(7);
5593        let victim = DetTid::from_raw(9);
5594        register_known_thread(&mut sched, keep);
5595        register_known_thread(&mut sched, victim);
5596        sched.runqueue_push_back(victim); // already queued; to be removed
5597        assert!(!sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress());
5598
5599        // No window live, yet both mutations buffer rather than apply.
5600        sched.admit_to_run_queue(keep, AdmitIntent::Fixed(AdmitSide::Back));
5601        sched.deschedule_or_defer(victim);
5602        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.contains_key(&keep));
5603        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.contains_key(&victim));
5604        assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(keep), "admission deferred");
5605        assert!(sched.run_queue.contains_tid(victim), "removal deferred");
5606
5607        // The daemon applies them at the drain (removals first, then admissions).
5608        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_removals();
5609        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions();
5610        assert!(sched.run_queue.contains_tid(keep));
5611        assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(victim));
5612        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.is_empty());
5613        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty());
5614    }
5615
5616    /// A thread admitted and then killed before the drain must end up neither
5617    /// queued nor pending: removals drain first and the retired-thread skip in
5618    /// the admission drain drops the buffered admission for a thread with no
5619    /// next_turns entry.
5620    #[test]
5621    fn buffered_admission_cancelled_by_buffered_removal() {
5622        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
5623        let tid = DetTid::from_raw(11);
5624        register_known_thread(&mut sched, tid);
5625
5626        sched.admit_to_run_queue(tid, AdmitIntent::Fixed(AdmitSide::Back));
5627        sched.deschedule_or_defer(tid);
5628
5629        // The thread is retired before the drain: its next_turns entry is gone.
5630        sched.next_turns.remove(&tid);
5631        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_removals();
5632        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions();
5633
5634        assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(tid));
5635        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.is_empty());
5636        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty());
5637    }
5638
5639    /// A successful nonleader exec retires the old process leader and reuses
5640    /// its raw TID for the caller's replacement image.  The old-leader removal
5641    /// and replacement-leader admission therefore share a `DetTid`, but they do
5642    /// not name the same thread incarnation: the drain must remove the former
5643    /// without cancelling the latter.
5644    #[test]
5645    fn nonleader_exec_removal_preserves_replacement_admission() {
5646        let config = Config {
5647            cancel_killed_thread_rpcs: true,
5648            backend_requires_thread_directed_process_signals: true,
5649            ..Config::default()
5650        };
5651        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&config);
5652        let leader = DetTid::from_raw(17);
5653        let caller = DetTid::from_raw(18);
5654        let detpid = DetPid::from_raw(leader.as_raw());
5655        let pre_exec_mm = MmId::initial(detpid);
5656
5657        sched.thread_tree.add_child(leader, leader, true);
5658        sched.thread_tree.add_child(leader, caller, false);
5659        register_known_thread(&mut sched, leader);
5660        register_known_thread(&mut sched, caller);
5661        sched.runqueue_push_back(leader);
5662        sched.runqueue_push_back(caller);
5663
5664        let old_leader_request = sched.next_turns.get(&leader).unwrap().req.clone();
5665        let post_exec_mm = pre_exec_mm.for_exec(detpid);
5666        let physical_pid = std::process::id() as i32;
5667        let physical_tid = unsafe { libc::syscall(libc::SYS_gettid) as i32 };
5668        sched
5669            .register_physical_thread(leader, pre_exec_mm, physical_pid, physical_tid)
5670            .expect("old leader PIDFD_THREAD registration must succeed");
5671        sched
5672            .register_physical_thread(leader, post_exec_mm, physical_pid, physical_tid)
5673            .expect("post-exec PIDFD_THREAD registration must replace the old address space");
5674        let retired = sched.reconnect_after_exec(ExecReconnect {
5675            caller,
5676            new_leader: leader,
5677            detpid,
5678            pre_exec_mm,
5679            post_exec_mm,
5680            child_tid_addr: 0,
5681            reconnect_priority: Some(DEFAULT_PRIORITY),
5682        });
5683
5684        assert_eq!(retired, vec![leader, caller]);
5685        assert!(matches!(old_leader_request.try_read(), Some(Err(_))));
5686        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.contains_key(&leader));
5687        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.contains_key(&leader));
5688        assert_eq!(
5689            sched.physical_thread_identity(leader),
5690            Some((post_exec_mm, physical_pid, physical_tid)),
5691            "old-leader cleanup must not remove the replacement leader's PIDFD_THREAD"
5692        );
5693
5694        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_removals();
5695        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions();
5696
5697        assert_eq!(
5698            sched
5699                .run_queue
5700                .tids()
5701                .filter(|dettid| **dettid == leader)
5702                .count(),
5703            1,
5704            "the first eligible drain must contain exactly one replacement leader"
5705        );
5706        assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(caller));
5707        assert!(sched.next_turns.contains_key(&leader));
5708        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.is_empty());
5709        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty());
5710    }

5712    #[test]
5713    fn exec_replacement_killed_before_drain_is_not_resurrected() {
5714        let config = Config {
5715            cancel_killed_thread_rpcs: true,
5716            ..Config::default()
5717        };
5718        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&config);

5720        let caller = DetTid::from_raw(18);
5721        let (detpid, pre_exec_mm, _) = install_runnable_exec_group(&mut sched, leader, caller);
5722
5723        reconnect_nonleader_exec(&mut sched, leader, caller, detpid, pre_exec_mm);

5725            sched.pending_run_queue_removals.get(&leader),
5726            Some(&RemovalDisposition::ReplaceThenAdmit)
5727        );
5728
5729        sched.logically_kill_thread(&leader, &detpid, pre_exec_mm.for_exec(detpid));
5730        assert_eq!(
5731            sched.pending_run_queue_removals.get(&leader),

5733        );
5734        assert!(!sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.contains_key(&leader));
5735
5736        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_removals();
5737        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions();
5738
5739        assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(leader));
5740        assert!(!sched.next_turns.contains_key(&leader));
5741        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_admissions.is_empty());
5742        assert!(sched.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty());

5745    #[test]
5746    fn exec_reconnect_only_buffers_while_tentative_selection_is_live() {
5747        let config = Config {
5748            cancel_killed_thread_rpcs: true,
5749            ..Config::default()
5750        };
5751        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&config);
5752        let anchor = DetTid::from_raw(3);
5753        let leader = DetTid::from_raw(17);
5754        let caller = DetTid::from_raw(18);
5755        register_known_thread(&mut sched, anchor);
5756        sched.runqueue_push_back(anchor);
5757        let (detpid, pre_exec_mm, _) = install_runnable_exec_group(&mut sched, leader, caller);
5758
5759        assert_eq!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_next(), Some(anchor));
5760        let queue_during_window = sched.run_queue.tids().copied().collect::<Vec<_>>();
5761
5762        reconnect_nonleader_exec(&mut sched, leader, caller, detpid, pre_exec_mm);
5763
5764        assert!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress());
5765        assert_eq!(
5766            sched.run_queue.tids().copied().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
5767            queue_during_window,
5768            "the reconnect handler must not mutate a tentatively selected queue"
5769        );
5770        assert_eq!(
5771            sched.pending_run_queue_removals.get(&leader),
5772            Some(&RemovalDisposition::ReplaceThenAdmit)
5773        );
5774
5775        sched.run_queue.undo_tentative_pop();
5776        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_removals();
5777        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions();
5778
5779        assert_eq!(
5780            sched
5781                .run_queue
5782                .tids()
5783                .filter(|dettid| **dettid == leader)
5784                .count(),
5785            1
5786        );
5787        assert!(sched.run_queue.contains_tid(anchor));
5788        assert!(!sched.run_queue.contains_tid(caller));
5789    }
5790
5791    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
5792    enum ExecReconnectTiming {
5793        BeforeDaemon,
5794        AfterCallerWait { yields: usize },
5795        CallerResolvedBeforeReconnect,
5796    }
5797
5798    #[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
5799    struct ExecDrainObservation {
5800        queue: Vec<DetTid>,
5801        next_post_fork_draw: bool,
5802        turn: u64,
5803        old_leader_registration_survived: bool,
5804    }
5805
5806    async fn observe_exec_reconnect_drain(timing: ExecReconnectTiming) -> ExecDrainObservation {
5807        let config = Config {
5808            sched_seed: Some(0x5107),
5809            runs_post_fork: RunsPostFork::Random,
5810            cancel_killed_thread_rpcs: true,
5811            ..Config::default()
5812        };
5813        let sched = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Scheduler::new(&config)));
5814        let global_time = Arc::new(Mutex::new(GlobalTime::new(&config)));
5815        let leader = DetTid::from_raw(17);
5816        let caller = DetTid::from_raw(18);
5817        let lower = DetTid::from_raw(31);
5818        let higher = DetTid::from_raw(37);
5819        let barrier_parent = DetTid::from_raw(99);
5820        let detpid = DetPid::from_raw(leader.as_raw());
5821        let pre_exec_mm = MmId::initial(detpid);
5822
5823        let (caller_request, old_leader_request) = {
5824            let mut s = sched.lock().unwrap();
5825            s.thread_tree.add_child(leader, leader, true);
5826            s.thread_tree.add_child(leader, caller, false);
5827            register_known_thread(&mut s, leader);
5828            register_known_thread(&mut s, caller);
5829            // Give the destroyed leader a visibly different queue band. The
5830            // replacement must use the caller's priority at the ordinary tail.
5831            s.priorities.insert(leader, runqueue::FIRST_PRIORITY);
5832            s.runqueue_push_back(leader);
5833            s.runqueue_push_back(caller);
5834            s.next_turns
5835                .get(&leader)
5836                .unwrap()
5837                .req
5838                .put(Ok(Resources::new(leader)));
5839
5840            register_known_thread(&mut s, lower);
5841            register_known_thread(&mut s, higher);
5842            s.admit_to_run_queue(lower, AdmitIntent::PostFork(RunsPostFork::Random));
5843            s.admit_to_run_queue(higher, AdmitIntent::PostFork(RunsPostFork::Random));
5844            // `step2` drains first, then this unresolved barrier returns
5845            // `SkipTurn`, exposing the exact first-drain queue before step3 can
5846            // tentatively select or rotate it.
5847            s.vfork_barriers.insert(barrier_parent, None);
5848            (
5849                s.next_turns.get(&caller).unwrap().req.clone(),
5850                s.next_turns.get(&leader).unwrap().req.clone(),
5851            )
5852        };
5853
5854        if matches!(timing, ExecReconnectTiming::BeforeDaemon) {
5855            reconnect_nonleader_exec(
5856                &mut sched.lock().unwrap(),
5857                leader,
5858                caller,
5859                detpid,
5860                pre_exec_mm,
5861            );
5862        } else if matches!(timing, ExecReconnectTiming::CallerResolvedBeforeReconnect) {
5863            caller_request.put(Ok(Resources::new(caller)));
5864        }
5865
5866        let turn_sched = sched.clone();
5867        let turn_time = global_time.clone();
5868        let turn = tokio::spawn(async move {
5869            let last: Result<Resources, SkipTurn> = Err(SkipTurn);
5870            do_a_turn_blocking(turn_sched, turn_time, &last).await
5871        });
5872
5873        if let ExecReconnectTiming::AfterCallerWait { yields } = timing {
5874            let mut saw_waiter = false;
5875            for _ in 0..1_000 {
5876                if caller_request.to_string() == "<ivar HasWaiter>" {
5877                    saw_waiter = true;
5878                    break;
5879                }
5880                tokio::task::yield_now().await;
5881            }
5882            assert!(saw_waiter, "daemon never waited on the exec caller request");
5883            for _ in 0..yields {
5884                tokio::task::yield_now().await;
5885            }
5886            reconnect_nonleader_exec(
5887                &mut sched.lock().unwrap(),
5888                leader,
5889                caller,
5890                detpid,
5891                pre_exec_mm,
5892            );
5893        }
5894
5895        assert!(turn.await.expect("scheduler task panicked").is_err());
5896        let mut s = sched.lock().unwrap();
5897        let observation = ExecDrainObservation {
5898            queue: s.run_queue.tids().copied().collect(),
5899            next_post_fork_draw: s.child_runs_first_post_fork(RunsPostFork::Random),
5900            turn: s.turn,
5901            old_leader_registration_survived: s
5902                .next_turns
5903                .get(&leader)
5904                .is_some_and(|turn| turn.req == old_leader_request),
5905        };
5906        if !matches!(timing, ExecReconnectTiming::CallerResolvedBeforeReconnect) {
5907            assert_eq!(
5908                observation
5909                    .queue
5910                    .iter()
5911                    .filter(|tid| **tid == leader)
5912                    .count(),
5913                1
5914            );
5915            assert!(!observation.queue.contains(&caller));
5916            assert!(!observation.old_leader_registration_survived);
5917            assert!(s.pending_run_queue_admissions.is_empty());
5918            assert!(s.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty());
5919        }
5920        observation
5921    }
5922
5923    #[tokio::test]
5924    async fn exec_reconnect_caller_gate_fixes_first_drain_membership_and_prng() {
5925        let canonical = observe_exec_reconnect_drain(ExecReconnectTiming::BeforeDaemon).await;
5926        for yields in [0, 1, 64] {
5927            assert_eq!(
5928                canonical,
5929                observe_exec_reconnect_drain(ExecReconnectTiming::AfterCallerWait { yields }).await,
5930                "host delay of {yields} yields changed the first eligible drain"
5931            );
5932        }
5933
5934        let broken =
5935            observe_exec_reconnect_drain(ExecReconnectTiming::CallerResolvedBeforeReconnect).await;
5936        assert!(broken.old_leader_registration_survived);
5937        assert_ne!(
5938            canonical.queue, broken.queue,
5939            "the deliberate caller-gate violation was inert"
5940        );
5941    }
5942
5943    /// F6 (real-path regression): when the thread `step3_peek` tentatively
5944    /// selected dies while the daemon awaits its request, `do_a_turn_blocking`
5945    /// takes the `Err(ThreadExited)` fizzle arm. That arm MUST undo the tentative
5946    /// pop so the selection does not outlive the turn; otherwise the next pass's
5947    /// step2 removal drain calls `remove_tid` while `tentative_selection` is
5948    /// still `Some`, tripping the run queue's transaction guard -- the "reconnect
5949    /// panic moved one pass" defect (reachable in NORMAL async-DBT operation, not
5950    /// just the reviewed edge case: any thread that exits during the await window
5951    /// races here). This drives the ACTUAL async daemon function end to end
5952    /// rather than poking `RunQueue` directly, which is the coverage gap the
5953    /// pre-existing tests left. Positive control: after the fizzle the window is
5954    /// closed and the following real step2 removal drain does not panic.
5955    #[tokio::test]
5956    async fn reconnect_fizzle_closes_window_so_next_removal_drain_is_safe() {
5957        let config = Config::default();
5958        let sched = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Scheduler::new(&config)));
5959        let global_time = Arc::new(Mutex::new(GlobalTime::new(&config)));
5960        let dead = DetTid::from_raw(7);
5961        {
5962            let mut s = sched.lock().unwrap();
5963            s.priorities.insert(dead, DEFAULT_PRIORITY);
5964            s.next_turns.insert(
5965                dead,
5966                ThreadNextTurn {
5967                    dettid: dead,
5968                    child_tid_addr: 0,
5969                    // Request already resolved to `ThreadExited`: the daemon will
5970                    // observe the thread died the moment it awaits `req.get()`.
5971                    req: Ivar::full(Err(ThreadExited)),
5972                    resp: Ivar::new(),
5973                },
5974            );
5975            s.runqueue_push_back(dead);
5976        }
5977        let last: Result<Resources, SkipTurn> = Err(SkipTurn);
5978
5979        // Pass 1: step1 sees a filled request (quiescent), step3 tentatively
5980        // pops `dead`, then `req.get().await` yields `Err(ThreadExited)`. The
5981        // fizzle arm returns `SkipTurn` and undoes the tentative pop.
5982        let first = do_a_turn_blocking(sched.clone(), global_time.clone(), &last).await;
5983        assert!(first.is_err(), "fizzled reconnect turn skips");
5984        assert!(
5985            !sched.lock().unwrap().run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress(),
5986            "the ThreadExited arm must undo the tentative pop"
5987        );
5988
5989        // Pass 2: the dead thread's run-queue removal is now buffered, exactly
5990        // as a reconnect/kill handler would leave it. Because pass 1 closed the
5991        // window, the real step2 removal drain calls `remove_tid` with
5992        // `tentative_selection == None` and does NOT trip the guard. Pre-fix
5993        // (window left open) this call panicked.
5994        {
5995            let mut s = sched.lock().unwrap();
5996            s.deschedule_or_defer(dead);
5997            s.next_turns.remove(&dead);
5998            let _ = s.step2_process_blocked(&global_time);
5999            assert!(!s.run_queue.contains_tid(dead), "dead thread drained out");
6000            assert!(s.pending_run_queue_removals.is_empty());
6001        }
6002    }
6003
6004    /// F8 (non-committing fizzle), part 1: the branch itself, both ways.
6005    ///
6006    /// NEGATIVE -- the defect. A selected thread whose `next_turns` entry
6007    /// vanished during the daemon's post-await window must abandon the turn:
6008    /// report `SkipTurn` (it commits nothing -- steps 4-7 are bypassed) AND
6009    /// close the tentative window `step3_peek` opened. Before the fix this
6010    /// branch fell through to `Ok(rsrcs)`, which `bump_global_time` reads as a
6011    /// completed turn and answers with a virtual-time advance.
6012    /// POSITIVE -- not inert. A thread that is still registered proceeds: `Ok`,
6013    /// with its tentative selection left open for step4 to commit. Without this
6014    /// side an `abort_turn_if_thread_vanished` that simply always skipped would
6015    /// pass the negative and silently stall the scheduler.
6016    #[test]
6017    fn a_vanished_thread_aborts_its_turn_and_a_live_one_does_not() {
6018        let config = Config::default();
6019        let vanishing = DetTid::from_raw(11);
6020        let live = DetTid::from_raw(13);
6021
6022        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&config);
6023        register_known_thread(&mut sched, vanishing);
6024        register_known_thread(&mut sched, live);
6025        sched.runqueue_push_back(vanishing);
6026        sched.runqueue_push_back(live);
6027
6028        // NEGATIVE: retire `vanishing` after its selection was tentatively
6029        // popped, exactly as teardown does inside the post-await window.
6030        assert_eq!(
6031            sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_tid(vanishing),
6032            Some(vanishing)
6033        );
6034        assert!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress());
6035        sched.next_turns.remove(&vanishing);
6036        assert!(
6037            sched.abort_turn_if_thread_vanished(vanishing).is_err(),
6038            "a turn that bypasses steps 4-7 must report SkipTurn, never success: \
6039             reporting Ok lets bump_global_time advance virtual time for a turn \
6040             that committed nothing"
6041        );
6042        assert!(
6043            !sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress(),
6044            "the abandoned turn must also close its tentative window"
6045        );
6046
6047        // POSITIVE: a still-registered thread is untouched and proceeds to
6048        // step4 with its selection still open to commit.
6049        assert_eq!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_tid(live), Some(live));
6050        assert!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress());
6051        assert!(
6052            sched.abort_turn_if_thread_vanished(live).is_ok(),
6053            "a live thread's turn must proceed"
6054        );
6055        assert!(
6056            sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress(),
6057            "a proceeding turn must keep its tentative selection open for step4"
6058        );
6059    }
6060
6061    /// F8 (non-committing fizzle must not advance virtual time): both sides of
6062    /// the consequence, stated directly against `bump_global_time` -- the code
6063    /// that turns a turn's returned `Result` into a virtual-time decision.
6064    ///
6065    /// The defect: the fizzle arm where `req.get()` resolved `Ok` but the
6066    /// thread's `next_turns` entry vanished before the daemon re-acquired the
6067    /// lock skips steps 4-7, commits nothing, and used to fall through to
6068    /// `Ok(rsrcs)` -- landing in the advancing branch below and adding a
6069    /// DETLOG-visible tick for work that never happened. It now reports
6070    /// `Err(SkipTurn)` like its `ThreadExited` sibling, so this pair of
6071    /// assertions is what that fix buys.
6072    ///
6073    /// Deliberately NOT an end-to-end test of that arm. Reaching it requires
6074    /// teardown to clear `next_turns` inside the host-scheduling gap between the
6075    /// await resolving and the re-lock, and that gap is not constructible from a
6076    /// test: `step1_check_quiescence` only proceeds once every thread's request
6077    /// is already filled, so `req.get()` never suspends and there is no window a
6078    /// test can hold open. An attempt to win the lock in that gap is a genuine
6079    /// race that would usually lose (and deadlocks outright on a current-thread
6080    /// runtime, where the daemon's blocking `sched.lock()` stalls the whole
6081    /// executor). F6's `reconnect_fizzle_closes_window_so_next_removal_drain_is_
6082    /// safe` does drive the real `do_a_turn_blocking` for the sibling arm.
6083    ///
6084    /// NEGATIVE: a skipped turn leaves virtual time exactly where it was. That is
6085    /// what the F8 fix buys; before it, the non-committing arm reported `Ok` and
6086    /// landed in the advancing branch.
6087    /// POSITIVE: a committed, non-internal turn *does* advance it. Without this
6088    /// side the negative would pass vacuously for a `bump_global_time` that had
6089    /// simply stopped advancing time at all.
6090    #[test]
6091    fn virtual_time_advances_for_a_committed_turn_and_not_for_a_skipped_one() {
6092        let config = Config::default();
6093        let runnable = DetTid::from_raw(13);
6094
6095        // A non-empty run queue keeps the "only waiting on external events"
6096        // guard from suppressing the advance for an unrelated reason.
6097        let mut advancing = Scheduler::new(&config);
6098        advancing.priorities.insert(runnable, DEFAULT_PRIORITY);
6099        advancing.runqueue_push_back(runnable);
6100        let advancing_time = Mutex::new(GlobalTime::new(&config));
6101        let before_commit = advancing_time.lock().unwrap().as_nanos();
6102        advancing.bump_global_time(&advancing_time, &Ok(Resources::new(runnable)));
6103        let after_commit = advancing_time.lock().unwrap().as_nanos();
6104        assert!(
6105            after_commit > before_commit,
6106            "a committed turn must advance virtual time ({before_commit:?} -> {after_commit:?})"
6107        );
6108
6109        let mut skipping = Scheduler::new(&config);
6110        skipping.priorities.insert(runnable, DEFAULT_PRIORITY);
6111        skipping.runqueue_push_back(runnable);
6112        let skipping_time = Mutex::new(GlobalTime::new(&config));
6113        let before_skip = skipping_time.lock().unwrap().as_nanos();
6114        skipping.bump_global_time(&skipping_time, &Err(SkipTurn));
6115        let after_skip = skipping_time.lock().unwrap().as_nanos();
6116        assert_eq!(
6117            after_skip, before_skip,
6118            "a skipped turn must not advance virtual time"
6119        );
6120    }
6121
6122    /// Liveness (negative control) for the F6 fix above: proves the guard the
6123    /// fizzle arm protects is real, so the positive test is not vacuous. If a
6124    /// fizzled turn does NOT undo its tentative pop (the pre-fix behavior), the
6125    /// next pass's step2 removal drain calls `remove_tid` while
6126    /// `tentative_selection` is `Some`, and the run queue's transaction guard
6127    /// panics. This is precisely the panic `undo_tentative_pop` prevents.
6128    #[test]
6129    #[should_panic(expected = "tentative_selection.is_none()")]
6130    fn removal_drain_panics_if_tentative_window_left_open() {
6131        let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
6132        let dead = DetTid::from_raw(7);
6133        register_known_thread(&mut sched, dead);
6134        sched.runqueue_push_back(dead);
6135
6136        // Simulate step3_peek selecting `dead` with the turn neither committing
6137        // nor undoing -- i.e., the fizzle arm WITHOUT its undo.
6138        assert_eq!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_next(), Some(dead));
6139        assert!(sched.run_queue.tentative_pop_in_progress());
6140
6141        // Next pass buffers the removal and drains: remove_tid trips the guard.
6142        sched.deschedule_or_defer(dead);
6143        sched.next_turns.remove(&dead);
6144        sched.drain_pending_run_queue_removals();
6145    }
6146
6147    /// F7 (adjacent-snapshot sensitivity): this fixture directly places two
6148    /// admissions in adjacent step2 drains; it does not model a production
6149    /// handler protocol. Every current asynchronous admission site separately
6150    /// fixes snapshot membership: ordinary clone buffers before the parent's
6151    /// `ParentContinue`, `vfork` uses its registration barrier, and exec
6152    /// reconnect buffers before retiring and resolving the caller request. See
6153    /// [`Scheduler::admit_to_run_queue`] for those causal bindings.
6154    ///
6155    /// The synthetic split remains a negative/sensitivity bracket for that
6156    /// requirement. Replaying one fixed split at one seed is reproducible, but
6157    /// swapping which child occupies the first drain changes the resolved queue
6158    /// order. Thus a future unanchored admission site would make host-selected
6159    /// membership observable; this test must not be read as evidence that any
6160    /// current production site is unanchored.
6161    #[test]
6162    fn deferred_admission_binds_to_snapshot_membership_across_adjacent_drains() {
6163        let config = Config {
6164            sched_seed: Some(0x5107),
6165            runs_post_fork: RunsPostFork::Random,
6166            ..Default::default()
6167        };
6168        let anchor = DetTid::from_raw(3);
6169        let a = DetTid::from_raw(31);
6170        let b = DetTid::from_raw(37);
6171
6172        // Admit `first` in drain 1 and `second` in drain 2 (two adjacent step2
6173        // drains) and return the final run-queue order relative to a fixed
6174        // anchor, which encodes each child's resolved front/back side.
6175        let split = |first: DetTid, second: DetTid| -> Vec<DetTid> {
6176            let mut sched = Scheduler::new(&config);
6177            register_known_thread(&mut sched, anchor);
6178            register_known_thread(&mut sched, a);
6179            register_known_thread(&mut sched, b);
6180            sched.runqueue_push_back(anchor);
6181
6182            sched.admit_to_run_queue(first, AdmitIntent::PostFork(RunsPostFork::Random));
6183            sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions(); // drain 1
6184            sched.admit_to_run_queue(second, AdmitIntent::PostFork(RunsPostFork::Random));
6185            sched.drain_pending_run_queue_admissions(); // drain 2 (adjacent)
6186
6187            sched.run_queue.tids().copied().collect()
6188        };
6189
6190        // Fixed synthetic membership is deterministic across identical replays.
6191        let canonical = split(a, b);
6192        assert_eq!(
6193            canonical,
6194            split(a, b),
6195            "identical schedule -> identical result"
6196        );
6197        assert!(canonical.contains(&a) && canonical.contains(&b) && canonical.contains(&anchor));
6198
6199        // Sensitivity control: changing synthetic snapshot membership changes
6200        // the outcome for this seed, so a missing production anchor would be
6201        // observable rather than inert.
6202        assert_ne!(
6203            split(a, b),
6204            split(b, a),
6205            "swapping snapshot membership changes the resolved order"
6206        );
6207    }

6210    fn vfork_registration_barrier_blocks_until_child_registration() {
6211        let mut scheduler = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
6212        let parent = DetTid::from_raw(3);
6213        let child = DetTid::from_raw(5);
6214        scheduler.vfork_barriers.insert(parent, None);
6215
6216        assert!(scheduler.step2a_wait_for_vfork_barrier().is_err());
6217        scheduler.complete_vfork_registration(parent, child);
6218        assert!(scheduler.step2a_wait_for_vfork_barrier().is_ok());
6219        assert_eq!(scheduler.vfork_barriers.get(&parent), Some(&Some(child)));
6220    }

6245    // AUTONOMOUS-BOT-IMPLEMENTED
6246    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-1152): Review deferred vfork child registration.
6247    #[test]
6248    fn vfork_registration_barrier_waits_for_deferred_child_at_continuation() {
6249        // On a backend that defers the child spawn (e.g. KVM), the parent posts its continuation
6250        // BEFORE the child registers. An unfulfilled barrier at continuation must be kept, not
6251        // torn down as a failed clone; otherwise the late child panics on registration.
6252        let config = Config {
6253            backend_defers_vfork_child_registration: true,
6254            ..Config::default()
6255        };
6256        let mut scheduler = Scheduler::new(&config);
6257        let parent = DetTid::from_raw(3);
6258        let child = DetTid::from_raw(5);
6259        let op_id = ExternalOpId::new(parent, 7);
6260        let mut continuation = Resources::new(parent);
6261        continuation.insert(ResourceID::BlockedExternalContinue(op_id), Permission::RW);
6262
6263        scheduler.vfork_barriers.insert(parent, None);
6264        scheduler.next_turns.insert(
6265            parent,
6266            ThreadNextTurn {
6267                dettid: parent,
6268                child_tid_addr: 0,
6269                req: Ivar::full(Ok(continuation)),
6270                resp: Ivar::new(),
6271            },
6272        );
6273
6274        // Parent is at its continuation but the child has not registered: keep waiting, keep the
6275        // barrier (the failed-clone teardown must NOT fire on a deferring backend).
6276        assert!(scheduler.step2a_wait_for_vfork_barrier().is_err());
6277        assert_eq!(scheduler.vfork_barriers.get(&parent), Some(&None));
6278
6279        // The deferred child registers; now the barrier is fulfilled and released.
6280        scheduler.complete_vfork_registration(parent, child);
6281        assert!(scheduler.step2a_wait_for_vfork_barrier().is_ok());
6282        assert!(scheduler.vfork_barriers.is_empty());
6283    }

6285    // TODO-HUMAN-REVIEW(PR-1152): Review failed deferred-vfork cancellation.
6286    #[test]
6287    fn vfork_registration_barrier_releases_deferred_failed_clone() {
6288        let config = Config {
6289            backend_defers_vfork_child_registration: true,
6290            ..Config::default()
6291        };
6292        let mut scheduler = Scheduler::new(&config);
6293        let parent = DetTid::from_raw(3);
6294        let op_id = ExternalOpId::new(parent, 7);
6295        let mut failure = Resources::new(parent);
6296        failure.insert(ResourceID::VforkFailed(op_id), Permission::RW);
6297
6298        scheduler.vfork_barriers.insert(parent, None);
6299        scheduler.blocked.external_io_blockers.insert(parent, op_id);
6300        scheduler.next_turns.insert(
6301            parent,
6302            ThreadNextTurn {
6303                dettid: parent,
6304                child_tid_addr: 0,
6305                req: Ivar::full(Ok(failure)),
6306                resp: Ivar::new(),
6307            },
6308        );
6309
6310        // The explicit failure proves that no deferred child is coming, so step2a cancels the
6311        // barrier instead of waiting forever. The ordinary external-continuation path can then
6312        // requeue the parent, whose syscall handler still owns and returns the original errno.
6313        assert!(scheduler.step2a_wait_for_vfork_barrier().is_ok());
6314        assert!(scheduler.vfork_barriers.is_empty());
6315        assert!(scheduler.step2c_process_io_blockers().is_ok());
6316        assert!(scheduler.blocked.external_io_blockers.is_empty());
6317        assert!(scheduler.run_queue.contains_tid(parent));
6318    }

6333            .blocked

6337            signal_waiter,

6412    #[test]
6413    fn futex_wake_bitset_only_selects_intersecting_waiters() {
6414        let mut waiters = vec![
6415            futex_waiter(1, 0b0001),
6416            futex_waiter(2, 0b0010),
6417            futex_waiter(3, 0b0011),
6418        ];
6419
6420        let matching = take_matching_futex_waiters(&mut waiters, 0b0010);
6421        assert_eq!(
6422            matching
6423                .iter()
6424                .map(|waiter| waiter.dettid)
6425                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
6426            [DetTid::from_raw(2), DetTid::from_raw(3)]
6427        );
6428        assert_eq!(
6429            waiters
6430                .iter()
6431                .map(|waiter| waiter.dettid)
6432                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
6433            [DetTid::from_raw(1)]
6434        );
6435
6436        let matching = take_matching_futex_waiters(&mut waiters, 0);
6437        assert!(
6438            matching.is_empty(),
6439            "a zero wake bitset must match no waiter"
6440        );
6441        assert_eq!(waiters.len(), 1, "nonmatching waiters must remain queued");
6442    }

6463        let p1 = DetPid::from_raw(100);
6464        let p2 = DetPid::from_raw(200);

6484    #[test]
6485    fn pending_signal_does_not_rewrite_other_internal_pollers() {
6486        let mut scheduler = Scheduler::new(&Config::default());
6487        let target = DetTid::from_raw(100);
6488        register_known_thread(&mut scheduler, target);
6489        let mut polling = Resources::new(target);
6490        polling.insert(ResourceID::InternalIOPolling, Permission::W);
6491        polling.set_signal_interrupt_errno(Errno::EINTR);
6492        polling.fyi("poll");
6493        scheduler.next_turns.get_mut(&target).unwrap().req = Ivar::full(Ok(polling));
6494        scheduler.runqueue_push_back(target);
6495
6496        scheduler.notify_signal_pending(target, SigWrapper::from(Signal::SIGUSR1));
6497
6498        assert!(scheduler.pending_cross_task_signals.is_empty());
6499        assert!(scheduler.run_queue.contains_tid(target));
6500        assert!(scheduler.inbound_signals(target).is_empty());
6501
6502        scheduler.drain_pending_cross_task_signals();
6503
6504        assert!(scheduler.run_queue.contains_tid(target));
6505        assert!(scheduler.inbound_signals(target).is_empty());
6506        let resources = scheduler
6507            .next_turns
6508            .get(&target)
6509            .and_then(|next_turn| next_turn.req.try_read())
6510            .and_then(Result::ok)
6511            .expect("the target should hold its signal interruption request");
6512        assert_eq!(resources.resources.len(), 1);
6513        assert!(
6514            resources
6515                .resources
6516                .contains_key(&ResourceID::InternalIOPolling)
6517        );
6518    }
